<!-- page 1 -->
NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS’ MINUTES 
TUESDAY, August 14, 2012 
 
PRESENT: David Tenney, Supervisor; Jesse Thompson, Supervisor; Jonathan M. Nez, Supervisor; Jerry 
Brownlow, Supervisor; and J.R. DeSpain, Chairman were present.  
Chairman DeSpain acknowledged the elected officials in the audience.  
9:08 a.m.  Call To Order: Pledge of Allegiance and Invocation was led by Supervisor Thompson.  
CALL TO THE PUBLIC : Mary Springer introduced Lena Alley  who is taking John Swanson’s position in 
Finance. June Call addressed the Board regarding the Heber Overgaard Chamber of Commerce and tha nked 
everyone involved for all the support of the Tall Timbers Park. Supervisor Tenney stated that it is a good 
partnership and thanked M rs. Call. Supervisor Thompson addressed the Board on behalf of the Senior Centers 
and Elderly Programs in the White Cone Community. He presented the Board with plaque. He further stated 
that today is National Code Talkers Day. James Menlove reported to the Board that the County has received 
from the Government Finance Officers Association the prestigious award for our Comprehensive Annual 
Financial Report.  
CONSENT AGENDA:  a) Minutes: July 24, 2012  b) Warrant & Voucher Report over $1000, July 2012  c) 
Constable Reports: Holbrook Precinct #1, June 2012; Snowflake Precinct #3, Jul y 2012, and Show Low 
Precinct #5 July 2012  d) Justice Court Reports: Holbrook Precinct #1, July 2012; Winslow Precinct #2, July 
2012; Snowflake Taylor Precinct #3, July 2012; Kayenta Precinct #4, July 2012; Show Low Precinct #5, July 
2012; and Pinetop -Lakeside Precinct #6, July 2012  e) Clerk of Superior Court Report July 2012  f) Juvenile 
Probation Report July 2012  g) Tax Exemptions filed pursuant to A.R.S. §42 -11153 as a Request for 
Redemption of Waiver for 2 properties  h) Application for a Special Event Liquor License requested for the 
Summer Lang Cancer Fund  to be held on Saturday, October 13, 2012 at The Gathering Place, 4756 Buck 
Springs Road, Pinetop, AZ as requested by Taira Flowers  i) Application for Special Event Liquor License 
requested for the Hebe r-Overgaard Chamber of Commerce Oktoberfest to be held on September 15 and 16 at 
the Tall Timbers Park in Overgaard j) Intergovernmental Agreement for Regional Connector Transit Services k) 
Conduct a precinct-based Recall Election for Linda Stoffer, board member of the Sun Valley Fire District to be 
held on March 13, 2013 l) Enter into a Lease Contract for two new Konica Bizhub copiers for the Sheriff’s/Jail 
with National Bank of Arizona, Division of Zions First National Bank for approximately $16,700.17 pa id over 
36 months at 1.76% with $1.00 end buy  m) Transfer/Donation of a used Lanier LD445C Copier/Scanner/Fax 
machine (L9066920064) to the Navajo County Fair, Inc. for the value of $1.00  n) Fiscal Year 2011 -2012 
transfers o) Agreement for Annual Contributi ons to Navajo County Senior Centers p) Letter of Agreement 
between Charles Backus and Navajo County for fencing a section of Porter Mountain Road  q) Attorney 
General’s Office Agreement for $19,250 for the Victims’ Rights program  r) Acknowledgement of Annua l 
Report of Collections and Delinquencies and Settlement of Accounts -Pulled from the Consent agenda for 
discussion by the Treasurer  s) Cooperative Agreement for Animal Impoundment Services between Navajo 
County and the Humane Society of the White Mountains  t) Sympathy letter to the family of Snowflake Town 
Councilmember Larry Matyas  u) Sympathy letter to Mary Tenney and the Tenney family  v) Appropriation of 
County Funds for the Fiscal Year 2012 -2013 for the Arizona Cooperative Extension : Supervisor Brownlow 
made a motion to authorize the Chairman to sign the items in the consent agenda as presented except for item 
r; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.

<!-- page 2 -->
ITEM R: Acknowledgement of Annual Report of Collections and Delinq uencies and Settlement of Accounts : 
Treasurer Hernandez apologized for completing the wrong agenda item request form. He indicated that he did 
intend for this item to be a regular item and not a consent item. Treasurer  Hernandez reviewed the Annual 
Report of Collections and Delinquencies and Settlement of Accounts . Supervisor Brownlow made a motion  
to approve the Annual Report as presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney . Vote unanimous 
approving the motion. Treasurer Hernandez reviewed the collection of taxes and the tax lien sale. Treasurer 
Hernandez requested authorization to invest and reinvest funds for 2012/2013 fiscal year. Supervisor 
Thompson made a motion  to approve the authorization to invest funds for 2012/2013 fiscal year; motion 
seconded by Supervisor Tenney. Supervisor Nez asked about the  Kayenta School District . Superintendent 
Morrow indicated that Peabody pays their  portion. Chairman DeSpain indicated this is real property and it is a 
zero balance. Mrs. Morrow indi cated that they are up to date in their totals. Vote  unanimous approving the 
motion. Mr. Hernandez discussed the interest rates , the financial crisis and the deep recession. He further 
discussed the response of the Federal R eserve, investment yields and pr udent investment strategy of the 
Treasurer’s Office.  
HUMAN RESOURCES:  
a. Personnel Service Awards : David Coolidge acknowledged Jo Lilene Sanchez 5 years; Janine Jones 5 
years; Laura Sanchez 5 years; Normand Cody 5 years; Michael Meeks 5 years; Rayleen Richa rds 5 
years; Susan Lefevre 10 years; Maxine Paddock 15 years; Maxine Tyler 15 years; and Bencita Bowman 
20 years, for their years of service to Navajo County.  
b. Consideration and possible approval of Personnel Actions : David Coolidge  reviewed the 43 item 
personnel action report. Supervisor Brownlow made a motion to approve the Personnel Action Report 
as presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Supervisor Tenney declared a conflict on item 39 as 
the employee is his daughter. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
ARIZONA ASSOCIATION OF COUNTIES: AACo Presentation 2012: Cammy Darris introduced members 
of the Arizona Association of Counties. Nicole Stickler introduced Trey Williams and Keith Russe ll. Keith 
Russell addressed the Board regarding discussion with county elected officials about their needs . Nicole 
Stickler gave a brief presentation of the AACo Assoc iation. She discussed the FY12 o utcomes and services 
provided to Navajo County touching on advocacy, cost sav ings programs and education. Trey Williams spoke 
of the advocacy outcomes from Arizona’s Centennial Legislature including session statistics and details. Nicole 
Stickler spoke about budget  and costs shifts. She spoke of the Arizona County Governments Platform  and the 
impact of the legislative solutions. She also reviewed some of the other value added programs and services to 
save money.  She continued to discuss AACo’s services . S he further spoke of the Horizon FY13 service 
expansions. Supervisor Thompson made comments. Mr. Jayne thanked Nicole, Mr. Russell, and Tre y for what 
they do for county government in Arizona. Mr. Jayne made comments about our Assessor Cammy Darris . Mr. 
Russell expressed his appreciation to Cammy Darris.   
NAVAJO COUNTY PUBLIC HEALTH SERVICES DISTRICT Board of Directors Session: 
Consideration and possible approval of Contract #ADHS13 -028371 with Arizona Department of Health 
Services for Maternal, Infant and Early Childhood Home Visiting Program effective August 1, 2012 to July 31, 
2017 in the amount of $102,000.00: Supervisor Nez made a motion to enter into the Navajo County Public 
Health Services District Board of Directors Session; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous 
approving the motion . Mary Tyler discussed the item. Supervisor Brownlow made a motion to approve 
Contract #ADHS13 -028371 with Arizona Department of Health Services for Maternal, Infant and Early

<!-- page 3 -->
Childhood Home Visiting Program effective August 1, 2012 to July 31, 2017 in the amount of $102,000.00 ; 
motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
SHERIFF: Consideration and possible approval to enter into Intergovernmental Agreement for participation in 
the Arizona Child Abduction Response Team (CART) : Sheriff KC Clark discussed the item. Supervisor 
Brownlow made a motion to approve the Intergovernmental Agreement for participation in the Arizona Child 
Abduction Response Team; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
FINANCE: Consideration and possible adoption of the fiscal year 2012 -2013 property tax rates for all 
applicable governmental entities within Navajo County as presented : Linda Morrow discussed the FY12 -13 
Navajo County public school district property tax rates.  Supervisor Brownlow made a motion to accept the 
tax rates as presented for the school districts in Navajo County; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney. Vote 
unanimous approving the motion. James Menlove discussed the one page county property tax rates. Supervisor 
Brownlow made a motion  to approve the primary tax rates and secondary tax rates for the 2012 -2013 as 
presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
WORKFORCE INVESTMENT ACT:  
a. Consideration and possible approval of the Intergovernmental Agreement, Contract #DE111011 -001 
Amendment 5 between the Arizona Department of Economic Security and Navajo County Board of 
Supervisors for WIA PY12/FY13 allocated funding in the amount of $508,320.00 : Judy Bratcher 
discussed the item. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to  approve the Intergovernmental Agreement, 
Contract#DE111011-001 Amendment 5 between the Arizona Department of Economic Security and 
Navajo County Board of Supervisors for WIA PY12/FY13 allocated funding in the amount of 
$508,320.00; motion seconded by Supervisor Brownlow. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. Consideration and possible approval of the Intergovernmental Agreement, Contract #DE111011 -001 
Amendment 6 between the Arizona Department of Economic Security and Navajo County Board of 
Supervisors for WIA PY11 incentive funding in the amount of $48,434 : Judy Bratcher discussed the 
item indicating that this is an incentive award for our work. Supervisor Brownlow asked about tribal 
projects. Judy indicated that all tribal entities have the ir own WIA programs however we do work with 
them. Supervisor Brownlow  made a motion to approve the Intergovernmental Agreement, Contract 
#DE111011-001 Amendment 6 between the Arizona Department of Economic Security and Navajo 
County Board of Supervisors fo r WIA PY11 incentive funding in the amount of $48,434 ; motion 
seconded by Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.   
c. Presentation on results from the Summer Youth 2012 Program: Judy Bratcher gave a presentation on the 
results from the Summer  Youth 2012 program indicating that there were 48 participants within 24 
employers. She did offer some of the feedback that was offered from the employers of the students. Don 
Berry, Chairman of the organization expressed his praise for the program. Mr. Ja yne thanked Judy and 
Carla and the WIA team and their partnership with DES and their help with the displaced workers at 
Catalyst. Chairman DeSpain thanked Judy for her compas sion and her hard work for the C atalyst 
employees.  
PUBLIC WORKS:  
a. Consideration a nd possible approval of the expenditure in an amount not to exceed $42,800.00 from 
District IV Special Road funds to cover 50% of the first year road imp rovements for Timberland Acres 
Special Road District #1: Homero Vela discussed the item. Barbara Miller  wrote a letter to the Board of 
Supervisors. Steve Burdick addressed the Board regarding the Road Board and asked for the support of 
the Board. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to approve the expenditure in an amount not to exceed

<!-- page 4 -->
$42,800.00 from District I V Special Road funds to cover 50% of the first year road improvements for 
Timberland Acres Special Road District #1 ; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez . Vote unanimous 
approving the motion.  
b. Consideration and possible approval of the expenditure in an amoun t not to exceed $1,750.00 from 
District I Special Road funds for the purchase of a cutting edge for the Kayenta Chapter : Homero Vela 
discussed the item. Supervisor Nez  made a motion  to approve the expenditure in an amount not to 
exceed $1,750.00 from District I Special Road funds for the purchase of a cutting edge for the Kayenta 
Chapter; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
c. Consideration and possible award of Contract #B12 -06-020 Pavement Markings to the lowest 
responsible bidder, Pavement Marking, Inc. : Homero Vela discussed the item. Supervisor Tenney  
made a motion to award Contract #B12-06-020 Pavement Markings to the lowest responsible bidder, 
Pavement Marking, Inc.; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
d. Consideration and possible adoption of Resolution Number 23 -12, approving the proposed Zone 
Change from R1 -10 to C -R for portions of parcel numbers 212 -17-009, 015B & 016 owned by Bob 
McQueen: Trent Larson discussed the item. Mr. Bob McQueen thanked Supervisor B rownlow for 
examining the site. Chairman DeSpain opened the public hearing, there being no comments, closed the 
public hearing. Supervisor Brownlow made a motion  to adopt Resolution Number 23-12, approving 
the proposed Zone Change from R1-10 to C-R for portions of parcel numbers 212 -17-009, 015B & 016 
owned by Bob McQueen ; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney . Vote unanimous approving the 
motion.  
e. Consideration and possible approval of project priorities and provide suppo rt letters for RAC Grant 
(Secure Rural Schools and Community Self Determination Action 2000 – Title II Special Projects on 
Federal Land) submittals for funding year 2012 : Homero Vela  discussed the item an d reviewed the 
special projects. Mr. Vela answered q uestions of the Board. Supervisor Brownlow asked about 
priorities, is there a list and has the Forest Service had any input. Homero indicated that they did not 
comment. Supervisor Tenney  made a motion to approve the  project priorities and provide support 
letters for RAC Grant (Secure Rural Schools and Community Self Determination Action 2000 – Title II 
Special Projects on Federal Land) submittals for funding year 2012 ; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Brownlow. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
FINANCE: Jason Moore recomme nded that the Board convene in e xecutive session for both item s A and B. 
Supervisor Brownlow made a motion to convene in executive session; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
a. EXECUTIVE SESSION pursuant to A.R.S. §38 -431.03(A)(3) for the Board to receive legal advice 
from its attorneys regarding an Intergovernmental Agreement between the Show Low Fire District and 
Navajo County for the hiring of a financial consultant  
b. EXECUTIVE SESSION pursuant to A.R.S. §38 -431.03(A)(3) for the Board to receive legal advice 
from its attorneys regarding a contract with PolicyLogic LLC and the Show Low Fire District for 
consulting services  
c. RECONVENE for discussion and possible Board action on proposed Intergovernmental Agreement 
between the Show Low Fire District and Navajo County for the hiring of a financial consultant : 
Supervisor Tenney made a motion to reconvene in regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Nez. Vote unanimous approving the moti on. Jason Moore asked for approval of the Intergovernmental 
Agreement. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to approve the Intergovernmental Agreement between 
the Show Low Fire District and Navajo County for the hiring of a financial consultant ; motion seconded 
by Supervisor Brownlow. Vote unanimous approving the motion.

<!-- page 5 -->
d. RECONVENE for discussion and possible Board action on proposed contract with PolicyLogic LLC 
and the Show Low Fire District for consulting services : Jason Moore recommended approval and 
introduced John Flynn. John Flynn gave some background information  about his qualifications. 
Supervisor Tenney made a motion  to approve a contract with  PolicyLogic LLC and the Show Low 
Fire District for consulting services ; motion seconded by Supervisor Brownlow . Vote unanimous 
approving the motion.  
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS: Consideration and possible approval of a Proclamation declaring August 2012 
as Child Support Awareness Month : Melissa Buckley discussed the item. Supervisor Tenney made a motion 
to approve the P roclamation declaring August 2012 as Child Support Awareness Month; motion seconded by 
Supervisor Brownlow. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE: Report from Board members, County Attorney and County Manager : 
Supervisor Tenney thanked everyone for their outpouring of love for his father. James Jayne spoke of the 
passing of Lewis Tenney.  
ADJOURN: At 12:38  p.m. Supervisor Nez made a motion to adjourn; motion seconded by  Supervisor 
Brownlow. Vote unanimous approving the motion.   
 
 
APPROVED: 
 
__________________________________ 
J.R. DeSpain, Chairman 
 
ATTEST:  
 
DATE: August 28, 2012 
 
Melissa W. Buckley - Clerk of the Board 
 
 
 
Electronic recording of the Board of Supervisors Meeting is available for review  
upon a Public Records Request
